巧新(1563 TT) Trading Buy

2026、 2027 年營運動能向上，投資建議 Trading Buy。  
投資建議 Trading Buy ：巧新(1563 TT) 努力進行轉型，半導體
新事業於 2H26 逐漸取得進展，且再生鋁出貨占比也明顯拉升，
帶動毛利率明顯改善。2026 年獲利表現回升且 2027 年獲利能
力續揚。惟考量近期汽車零組件族群評價不易拉升，投資建議
Trading Buy，目標價 76 元 (以 2027 年 EPS 及 PE 15 倍評價)。  
3Q26 本業獲利小幅增長，因業外收入挹注，整體獲利顯著季增；
預估 4Q26 獲利相對下降：3Q26 營收 20.16 億元，QoQ+1.83%、
YoY+18.48%。受惠於再生鋁採用比重提升且匯率相對有利，毛
利率由 2Q26 的 24.32%提升至 25.90%。但因運輸成本上漲，營
業費用同步增長，營業利益 QoQ+6.72%、 YoY+211.71%。因業
外認列大筆匯兌利益，稅率表現穩定。稅後純益 2.47 億元，
QoQ+247.56%、 YoY+478.06%， EPS 1.46 元。4Q26 方面，預估
營收 19.21 億元，QoQ-4.70%、 YoY+16.97%。獲利能力方面，
受惠於再生鋁採用率高，預估毛利率提升至 27.54%。因運費相
對下降，預估營業利益 QoQ+15.87%、 YoY+342.49%。因 3Q26
已認列大筆匯兌收益，若以保守角度看待 4Q26 業外貢獻，預估
稅後純益 1.67 億元，QoQ-32.69%、YoY 轉虧為盈，EPS 0.98 元。 
預估 2026 年獲利表現回升，2027 年獲利能力續揚：2026 年方
面，輪圈出貨小幅提升，而半導體相關零組件自 2H26 開始貢獻
初步營收。預估 2026 年營收 77.89 億元，YoY+11.63%。隨著再
生鋁採用率提升且營收規模增長，毛利率有望逐季提升，預估
2026 年毛利率提升至 24.97%，營業利益 YoY+43.13%。若業外
較無匯兌損失影響，預估稅後純益 6.16 億元，YoY+93.56%，
EPS 3.64 元。2027 年方面，根據車廠預示量，鋁圈出貨量將由
2026 年的 60 萬顆提升至 70 萬顆，有助於帶動車用產品營收增
長。而半導體營收占比有機會提升至 5~10%，將提供額外營收
動能。因再生鋁採用比重提升，且半導體產品毛利率相對較高，
2027 年毛利率及獲利能力有望優於 2026 年。預估 2027 年營收
92.77 億元，YoY+19.10%，稅後純益 8.56 億元，YoY+38.99%，
EPS 5.06 元。

  巧新(1563 TT) ESG：  
Environmental 環境保護：(1)鋁下腳料 100%回收，製成再生鋁棒，持續循環
再利用。(2)導入各式節能設備具體節能，已降低溫室氣體排放量。(3)屏東
廠自建太陽能光電。Social 社會責任：(1)獲得 ASI(鋁業管理倡議)績效標準
認證。(2)定期進行人權盡職調查與評估，流程和政策涵蓋重要的人權問題，
保障員工和活動參與者的安全。(3)響應地方政府公益活動。Governance 公
司治理：(1)風險管理依公司程序辦理，內控嚴謹，並設有專職稽核部門定期
查核。(2)將誠信經營納入新人教育訓練課程與人力資源政策中，設立明確
有效之獎懲及申訴機制。(3)每年定期發布 ESG 報告書。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 036: 2026-11-16_［QE解説］7～9月期実質GDPは年率▲0.1％とほぼ横ばいの結果（1次QE）_714aa7e4

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-16_［QE解説］7～9月期実質GDPは年率▲0.1％とほぼ横ばいの結果（1次QE）_714aa7e4.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

7～9 月期実質GDP は年率▲0.1％と 
ほぼ横ばいの結果（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）と、ほぼ横ばいの結果。
4～6月期（前期比＋0.4％）から減速し、経済の緩やかな拡大傾向が一服 
○ 個人消費が2四半期連続でマイナスとなったほか、輸出が一時的要因もあり小幅減となり、成長率
を下押し。一方、設備投資はAI関連需要の堅調さを背景にプラス転換し、成長率を押し上げ 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）は、ほぼ横ばい 
内閣府が本日公表した2026年7～9月期の「四半期別GDP速報（QE）」（ 1次
速報値）では、実質GDPが前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）と、ほぼ横ばいの
結果になった。4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.7％）から減速し、経済
の緩やかな拡大傾向が一服した格好だ（図表）。 
需要項目別にみると、内需の柱である個人消費の減少や、輸出の小幅減少
が成長率の下押し要因になった。一方、設備投資は堅調に拡大し、成長率を
押し上げた。その他では、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイ
ナス要因になったが、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要
因として現れ、両者の成長率に対する影響は概ね打ち消し合っている。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
図表 2026 年 7～9 月期 GDP（1 次速報）

(注) 図の項目別寄与度の合計は、誤差により実質 GDP に完全に一致しない。表の値は、言及がない限り実質前期比（％）、括弧内は実質 GDP
前期比に対する寄与度（％Pt）

2

個人消費は2四半期連続
で減少。根強い節約志向
や、耐久財の特需剥落が
影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.3％と、2四半期連続で減少した（4～6月期は同＋
0.0％から同▲0.3％に下方修正）。足元の消費の弱さの背景には、複合的な
要因があると考えられる。 
品目別の内訳をみると、衣料品などの半耐久財が前期比▲1.4％（4～6月
期：同＋0.4％）と、大幅に減少した。長雨などの天候不順の影響で、衣料
品の消費が弱含んだとみられる。 
食品・エネルギー・各種消耗品などの非耐久財は、前期比▲0.2％（ 4～6月
期：同▲1.9％ ）と 2四半期連続で減少した。物価高を背景とする根強い節約
志向が影響したようだ。今回のインフレ局面が本格化する前の2020年と比較
すると、必需品が多い非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6年半でおよそ1.3
倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上昇が、消費者の体
感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、購入数量抑制につながっている可
能性がある。 
また、耐久財が前期比▲3.5％（4～6月期：同＋3.6％）と大幅減に転じた
ことも、7～9月期の消費を下押しした。これは、自動車とエアコンの「特需」
剥落が原因だ。自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に
廃止されたことを受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていた
が、8月以降は再び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年
4月の省エネ基準引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要
が生じていたが、景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入
った後は一服した模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減
少もエアコンなど季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
サービス消費は前期比＋0.1％（4～6月期：同▲0.1％）とプラスに転じた
が、伸びは緩慢だ。イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、サービスや価格帯、顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
一方、設備投資は前期比＋1.6％と、3四半期ぶりに前期比プラスへ転じた。
4～6月期も同▲0.9％から同▲0.4％と、依然マイナスながらも上方修正され
た。 
設備投資の関連指標では、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財総供給指
数（除く輸送機械）や、第3次産業活動指数におけるソフトウェア業の実質
売上高が改善している。世界的なAIブームを背景とするデータセンター等の
AI関連投資や、人手不足を受けたソフトウェア導入などの省力化投資が影響
したとみられる。 
ただし、7～9月期の変化率には、前期発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特

### Page 3

3

許権の海外譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少）の反動も影響している。足
元の設備投資の伸びは実態よりもやや強い結果とみられ、多少割り引いて評
価する必要があろう。 
住宅投資は前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲1.1％（4～6月期：同▲0.6％ ）と 、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスになった。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～9月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上
昇による需要低迷に加えて、地震や台風などによる着工・進捗の遅れが発生
した可能性がある。 
民間在庫は成長率へのプ
ラス寄与が縮小 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、±0.0％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラス幅が縮小した。4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不
足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油
の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響が
一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラス 
公的需要は前期比＋1.8％（4～6月期：同▲0.9％）と、2四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスとなった。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じたことだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変動の寄与度
が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備蓄の減少が
小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公的在庫の減
少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.5％Ptとプラス寄与した。 
一方、その他の公的需要項目では、公共投資が前期比▲1.1％（4～6月期：
同▲0.6％）と2四半期連続のマイナスになった。台風等により土木関連工事
の進捗に遅れが発生した可能性がある。また、政府消費は前期比▲0.0％（ 4
～6月期：同＋1.5％）とほぼ横ばいにとどまった。4～6月期は高校授業料・
小学校給食費の無償化開始により高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした
特殊要因が剥落した。 
原油代替調達で輸入が大
幅に増加し、外需はマイ
ナス寄与に反転 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.2％（4～6月期：同＋1.6％）と小幅に減少し
た一方、輸入が同＋2.5％（4～6月期：同▲1.3％）と大幅に増加した。その
結果、実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.6％Pt（4～6月期：同＋
0.6％Pt）と、マイナスに反転した。 
輸入の大幅な増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化に
より4～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホ
ルムズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの
輸入量が確保されている。 
こうした輸入の大幅増加は、7～9月期のGDP成長率に対し▲0.5％Ptのマイ
ナス寄与になった。もっとも、先述したように代替調達の進展に伴って国家
備蓄原油の放出が抑制され、公的在庫変動が＋0.5％Ptのプラス寄与になっ

### Page 4

4

ており、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果は公的在庫変動によりほぼ相殺さ
れた。 
輸出は、米国関税影響で落ち込んだ2025年7～9月期以来、4四半期ぶりの
前期比マイナスになった。内訳をみると、サービス輸出が前期比▲7.1％（ 4
～6月期：同＋8.9％ ）と 急減したことが主因だ。前期の4～6月期に特許権の
海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げたが、7～9月期はその反動減が現れたた
めであり（これは先述した7～9月期の設備投資の押し上げ要因でもある）、
一時的な動きと言えよう。なお、インバウンド需要（非居住者家計の国内で
の直接購入）は、前期比▲2.4％（4～6月期＋1.6％）と減少した。日中対立
の継続による中国人訪日客の少なさや、航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の
伸び悩みが影響したとみられる。 
一方、財輸出は前期比＋2.4％（ 4～6月期：同▲0.8％）と堅調に回復した。
旺盛なAI関連需要を背景に、半導体・電子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸
出がけん引したようだ。今後はサービス輸出の特殊要因が剥落するなか、財
輸出の拡大傾向が続き、輸出は全体として増加基調をたどると考えられる。 
2026 年度後半の実質GDP
は小幅プラスと予想。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期のプラス成長から減速し、ほぼ
横ばいの結果になった。先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～
2027年3月）の実質GDPは、均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。
個人消費が精彩を欠く一方で、設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろ
う。2026年度全体の実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平
均水準が押し上げられることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％
弱とまずまずの成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。原油・ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、コア消費者物価指数（生鮮食品を除く総合）は前年比＋2％台後半まで
上昇すると予想される（電気・ガス料金支援の再開と、ガソリン補助金縮小
を考慮）。また、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを中心
とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、一時的に消費の抑制要因になろ
う。ただし、2026年12月～2027年2月に政府の電気・ガス料金支援が実施さ
れることで、2027年1～3月期を中心に消費が下支えされる見込みだ。 
年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調査）では、
2026年度の設備投資計画（ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投資額（除く
土地投資額））が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した（6月調査：同＋8.8％ ）。
生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、設備の不足感が
強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、設備投資は今後拡大傾向で推移して
いく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」（特定生産性向上

### Page 5

5

設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、また、投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。 
日銀の10月会合は、物価
目標到達を確認した歴史
的な日に。今後は、12月会
合で追加利上げを行う見
込み 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。事前に報じられていたように、今回会合で
日銀は「消費者物価の基調的な上昇率は2％の「物価安定の目標」と概ね整
合的な水準」との見解を示した。2％の「物価安定の目標」を定めた2013年
1月から数えて、13年9カ月でようやく物価目標に到達した格好だ。これまで
長きにわたり実施してきたデフレ脱却のための特別な金融政策が、ついに終
わりを迎えたという意味で、歴史的な日になったと言える。 
今後は、基調的なインフレ率を2％程度の水準で「定着」させるために、物
価上振れ・下振れのリスクを都度評価して金融政策を調整していくことにな
る。当面は、中東情勢、AI関連需要、為替相場の変動（円安圧力）などを背
景に物価上振れリスクが強い状況が続くと見込まれ、日銀は金融環境への影
響を確認しながら利上げを継続するだろう。 
次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％）は、次回12月17・18日会合と予想する。前
回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて「3カ月に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、
日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の「半年強に1回」から加速していることを印
象付けるものになろう。最終的には、2027年半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％
まで引き上げていくとみている。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。 
（https://www.mizuhobank.co.jp/corporate/mhri/research/report/pdf/outlook261002.pdf）

## Document 064: 2026年10月外贸数据点评：AI相关需求旺盛，进出口维持高增

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月外贸数据点评：AI相关需求旺盛，进出口维持高增.pdf

AI相关需求旺盛，进出口维持高增
——2026年 10月外贸数据点评
[Table_Industry]日期: shzqdatemark [Table_Summary] 主要观点
AI 相关需求旺盛，进出口维持高增
10月进出口增速维持高增。出口增速二季度以来维持强势，10月高位
小幅下降。从主要出口市场表现来看，对美出口有所回落，增速仍在
30%以上，对日本、东盟、欧盟出口增速均有所下降，对东盟的出口
增速高达 40%以上。从主要出口商品增速来看，除了通用设备、自动
化数据处理设备以及集成电路外均有所下滑，AI相关产品出口仍维持
高景气。进口增速同样有所加快，增速突破 40%。从进口金额来看，
原油、煤、铜、二极管、集成电路有所回升，其中自动化数据处理设
备虽有所下降，但增速高达 261%。AI相关产品的进口需求较为强
劲。本月顺差维持在高位，但由于进口增长更快，顺差高位回落。
促进贸易平衡发展
我国外贸在此前关税扰动下表现韧性，我们认为主要还是中国制造业

1 事件：10月外贸数据公布
2026年 10月，我国货物贸易（下同）进出口总值 4.87 万亿
元，增长 31.2%。其中，出口 2.79 万亿元，增长 28.7%；进口
2.08万亿元，增长 34.7%；贸易顺差 7127.76亿元。按美元计价，
10月我国进出口总值 7193.83亿美元，增长 37.9%。其中，出口
4123.3亿美元，增长 35.2%；进口 3070.53亿美元，增长 41.6%；
贸易顺差 1052.77亿美元。
2 事件解析：数据特征和变动原因
2.1 进出口维持高增
10 月进出口增速（美元计价，下同）维持高增，顺差仍在高
位。出口增速二季度以来维持强势，10 月高位小幅下降。从主要
出口市场表现来看，对美出口有所回落，增速仍在 30%以上，对
日本、东盟、欧盟出口增速均有所下降，对东盟的出口增速高达
40%以上。金砖四国方面，除巴西外均有所下降，但整体增速均
可观。从主要出口商品增速来看，除了通用设备、自动化数据处
理设备以及集成电路外均有所下滑，AI 相关产品出口仍维持高景

气。
进口增速同样有所加快，增速突破 40%。从进口数量来看，
主要进口商品增速除大豆、钢材以外均有所回升；从进口金额来
看，原油、煤、铜、二极管、集成电路有所回升，其中自动化数
据处理设备虽有所下降，但增速高达 261%。AI 相关产品的进口
需求较为强劲。

但增速可观，对东盟出口增速超过 40%，对美出口也超过了 30%。

持高景气。

2.4 顺差维持高位
10 月贸易顺差为 1052.77 亿美元，9 月为 1335.32 亿美元，
去年同期为 879.98亿美元。本月顺差维持在高位，但由于进口增
长更快，顺差高位回落。

3 事件影响：对经济和市场
3.1 AI 相关需求旺盛，进出口维持高增
10 月进出口增速维持高增。出口增速二季度以来维持强势，
10月高位小幅下降。从主要出口市场表现来看，对美出口有所回
落，增速仍在 30%以上，对日本、东盟、欧盟出口增速均有所下
降，对东盟的出口增速高达 40%以上。从主要出口商品增速来看，
除了通用设备、自动化数据处理设备以及集成电路外均有所下滑，
AI 相关产品出口仍维持高景气。进口增速同样有所加快，增速突
破 40%。从进口金额来看，原油、煤、铜、二极管、集成电路有
所回升，其中自动化数据处理设备虽有所下降，但增速高达 261%。
AI 相关产品的进口需求较为强劲。本月顺差维持在高位，但由于
进口增长更快，顺差高位回落。
4 事件预测：趋势判断
4.1 促进贸易平衡发展
我国外贸在此前关税扰动下表现韧性，我们认为主要还是中
国制造业在世界经济格局中的优势地位所决定的，作为全球制造

中心，这也是我国应对外部不确定性的优势，并且也得到了进一
步的加强巩固。我们认为外部环境的改善，中美关税继续暂停以

### Page 7

宏观数据
请务必阅读尾页重要声明 7
及全球 AI 产业链的发展，也推动了贸易的加速增长。政策方面，
根据 7 月政治局会议的安排，要拓展国际经贸互利合作空间，大
力发展服务贸易，促进贸易平衡发展；实施好更加积极的财政政
策和适度宽松的货币政策，充分发挥各项存量政策效能，及时谋
划出台务实管用的增量政策，加大逆周期调节力度，推动经济持
续向新向优向好发展，努力实现“十五五”良好开局。
5 风险提示：
地缘政治事件恶化，国际金融形势改变，中美政策超预期变
化。

## Document 122: 261112_daiwa_HH

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_daiwa_HH.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review Hon Hai’s 3Q26 results, announced 12 November. 
We are impressed with its opex control, which led to solid operating margin 
expansion despite gross margin dilution from rising AI server contribution.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat. Net income was TWD78bn (EPS: 
TWD5.53), 25% higher than our estimate (TWD62bn) and 10% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus (TWD70bn). Revenue was TWD3.0tn (+19% 
QoQ; +46% YoY) with better-than-expected smartphone contribution. The 
gross margin came in at 6.02% (vs. 6.12% in 2Q26), a 0.1pp decline QoQ 
on a rising cloud segment mix. That said, the operating margin expanded 
0.07pp to 3.83%, driven by strong opex control and better leverage.

Key updates: Hon Hai reaffirms its positive view of its 2027 revenue 
momentum, targeting double-digit growth YoY (similar to our forecast). The 
company believes its cloud business will remain a key growth driver (54% 
of total revenue in 3Q26). Regarding total AI server racks, Hon Hai expects 
to see high-double-digit growth in 2027 (vs. our expectation of c.30% YoY 
growth from GPU server plus a rising ASIC contribution; however, as ASIC 
AI servers typically utilise consignment models, its AI server revenue 
growth will be slightly diluted). As for smartphones, considering Hon Hai’s 
key clients’ models command high ASPs, we believe the impact from rising 
memory costs will be relatively smaller, but we expect the PC segment to 
decline YoY due to the negative impact from rising component costs.

During the 3Q26 results call, AI server end demand from the US was the 
hottest question, especially after the US mid-term elections. We believe 
management’s view on hyperscalers’ and neocloud’s near-term demand 
remains positive, but the long-term impact must be closely monitored. Also, 
as investors are worried about the AI server capex slowdown from CSPs, 
management also indicated that there would be new end demand from 
sovereign and enterprise groups, which could act as a second growth 
wave. For 4Q26, the company is guiding for >15% revenue growth QoQ 
and YoY given the ICT peak season along with rising AI server revenue (up 
by 5-15% QoQ due to ongoing AI server shipment strength).

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 10-12% to reflect 
higher ASP assumptions for AI servers. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating but 
trim the 12M TP to TWD320, using a lower target PER of 13x (prior 16x; vs. 
its past-3-year range of 8-18x) on our 1-year forward EPS, given the overall 
weaker sentiment. Downside risk: low demand for AI servers.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E revenue are above the consensus by 2-3%, 
likely as we are more optimistic on Hon Hai’s AI server shipments.

12 November 2026

3Q26 review: solid opex control

➢ OPM expansion on strong scale leverage despite GM contraction  
➢ Demand outlook remains promising even after US mid-term elections 
➢ Reiterating Buy (1) rating; trimming 12M TP to TWD320

Company profile 
Established in 1974, Hon Hai Precision Industry (Hon Hai) started out making plastic products, 
connector products and cable assemblies. It is now the largest electronics manufacturing service (EMS) 
provider globally in terms of design, manufacturing, global logistics and after-market service.

### Page 5

5

Hon Hai Precision Industry (2317 TT): 12 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 12 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Hon Hai has incorporated 4 independent members into its 7-member board of directors in pursuit
of better governance. However, the company's Chairman also serves concurrently as President,
implying an absence of separation between board leadership and executive management.
Capital management 1 Hon Hai has declared >40% payout ratio for most of the years since 2014, and >50% in the past 5 years, pointing to a shareholder-friendly dividend policy.
Related party &
transaction 2
Revenue from related parties accounted for 1.6% of total revenue for 2025, while purchases
from related parties constituted 0.1% of cost of sales. We believe there is limited risk from
related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2 Hon Hai adheres to green design concepts and incorporates environmental factors and pollution prevention measures during the design and development stages. The company's green product design guidelines include 4 axes: "energy-saving, non-harmful, reduction and easily recyclable."
S Labor practices 2
Hon Hai recognises employees as the most important asset and continues to invest in its
workforce. In terms of safety, Hon Hai has upgraded its occupational safety and health
management system since 2019, and obtained the ISO45001 and CNS45001 certificates. The
company held online activities related to factory safety and health. Overall, we note that Hon Hai
has improved in terms of labour management in recent years. Hon Hai established a team to
implement data security management. Moreover, it plans to conduct data security training and
education regularly for all employees. Hon Hai aims to achieve zero data security incident in the
future.
S Product design & lifecycle management 2 Hon Hai adheres to green design concepts and incorporates environmental factors and pollution prevention measures during the design and development stages. The company's green product design guidelines include 4 axes: "energy-saving, non-harmful, reduction and easily recyclable."
Company specific Management Analyst comments
E GHG emissions 2
In November 2020, Hon Hai announced its commitment to achieving net zero greenhouse gas
emissions by 2050. Its Scope 1 and 2 GHG emissions decreased by 53.6% in 2025 compared
with 2020, exceeding its reduction target of 21%. That said, the company's actual energy-saving
rate in mainland China was 5.2% in 2025, successfully achieving its energy-saving goal of 4.2%.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

E Ink Holdings

Cuts 2026 guidance again; PTFE mass 
production in 2028; reiterate Neutral

Reiterate Rating: NEUTRAL | PO: 148.00 TWD | Price: 140.00 TWD

Cuts ’26 sales guidance to +9% YoY; IoT as key ’27 driver  
Mgmt. cut its 2026 guidance again and now expects overall sales to +9% YoY (prior: up 
10-15% YoY; Jan-Oct sales +4% YoY). It expects IoT sales up 35%+ YoY (BofAe: +39% 
YoY, with ESL sales +32% and signage sales more than doubling) and CE sales -15% YoY. 
Mgmt. cited weaker CE demand and still expects signage at single-digit% of 2026 sales 
(BofAe: 7%). The latest full-year guidance implies a mid- to high single-digit% MoM 
decline in Nov and Dec. Mgmt. gave no numeric 2027 guidance, but it still sees ESL and 
signage (especially mid-size) as the key drivers, while CE order visibility stays low. 
PTFE in focus on 3Q26 call; mass production likely in ’28 
E Ink’s PTFE collaboration with Taiflex (8039 TT; not covered) was one of the main 
topics on the 3Q26 call. We now take a neutral view on E Ink’s PTFE business. We see its 
strong R&D capability in material modification and strength in roll-to-roll manufacturing, 
but mgmt. said the business model is still under discussion and mass production could 
occur in 2028. As a result, our estimates currently do not include any PTFE contribution. 
3Q26 recap: less-favorable mix weighs on margins 
OP was 21%/17% below BofAe/consensus as GM/OPM of 52.2%/26.6% came in lower 
than expected (6.1ppt/7.0ppt below BofAe). Mgmt. said the weaker GM came from a 
higher mix of lower-margin module business. NP was 20%/15% below BofAe/consensus. 
Reiterate Neutral; cut PO to NT$148 on lower P/E 
We cut our 2026E EPS by 8% but lift our 2027-28E EPS by 0-5% to reflect 3Q26 miss 
and the latest guidance. We trim our PO to NT$148 (12x 2027E EPS) from NT$185 (15x 
2027E EPS), with a lower multiple on reduced CE demand visibility and macro risk. 12x is 
similar to ESL system integrators’ P/E average. We reiterate our Neutral given moderate 
sales growth, still-expanding margins, and fair valuation (Exhibit 4).

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
E Ink is top global e-paper supplier with over 90% market 
share. E Ink was originally spun out of MIT Media Lab in 
1997 and was acquired by Prime View International in 
2009. The two companies were combined/branded as E Ink 
into e-paper supply chain. Besides its headquarters in 
Hsinchu (Taiwan), E Ink has facilities in Linkou (Taiwan), 
Yangzhou (China), and South Hadley (US).

Investment Rationale 
We have a Neutral rating on E Ink given moderate 2026 
guidance and 2027 sales growth with potential 
demand/order risk due to component shortage and macro 
uncertainty. Yet we still like E Ink's dominant position in 
global e-paper market with over 90% market share, high 
margins thanks to higher mix from better-margin IoT 
business, and positive OCF/FCF.

OBAL RESEARCH

Price objective basis & risk 
E Ink Holdings (PVWIF) 
We derive our PO of NT$148 from 12x 2027E EPS. Our target P/E multiple is set at the 
average of global key ESL system integrators. It is also at the lower end of 11-34x P/E 
range since AI era from 2023. We believe E Ink's valuation has been re-set with a lower 
premium after AI as its business is less directly exposed to AI.

Upside risks: (1) stronger market demand in consumer electronics, (2) faster ESL 
demand/adoption, (3) faster adoption and more applications in various vertical markets 
like smart transportation/city and smart medical, (4) more favorable USD/RMB 
movement to drive margin expansion and/or non-operating items, and (5) higher non-
operating royalty income due to stronger TFT-LCD demand.

Downside risks: (1) much weaker-than-expected consumer demand, (2) slower-than-
expected ESL demand/adoption to replace from conventional labels, (3) lower utilization 
rate to weigh on margins under higher capex and depreciation costs, (4) higher raw 
material cost and/or logistics cost, (5) lower non-operating royalty income due to weaker 
TFT-LCD demand, and (6) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to 
weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

### Page 1

M
Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
Server Ramp Takes Shape but
Valuation Keeps Us UW
Compal’s server expansion is progressing, with revenue
contribution rising to ~12% in 3Q and management targeting 30-
40% in CY27. However, weaker PC volumes and diminishing
scale benefits continue to pressure margins. At ~14x CY27E P/E,
valuation remains stretched.
3Q OpM missed. See our  3Q26 Results Summary                   .
PC business facing demand and profitability headwinds: Compal expects a mid-

teens q/q decline in 4Q26 shipments because of higher selling prices. This weakness
should persist into CY27; the company projects a likely double-digit y/y unit decline,
though it admitted that visibility remains low. However, rising ASPs from mix
changes and cost pass-through could keep 4Q PC revenue up sequentially.
Servers have become a key growth driver: Servers rose from ~5% of the mix in 1Q
to ~12% in 3Q, with a CY27 revenue contribution target of 30-40%. While 4Q26

server revenue is guided to be flat q/q, Compal is aggressively scaling its capacity. It
now primarily ships from Taiwan, and a major L10/11 ramp will begin in 2Q27 at its
new Texas plant. It currently does L10 HGX for Neocloud customers and L6 for a US
enterprise customer; it will do L10/11 for enterprise customer(s) as well, and is
actively approaching CSP customers for ASIC L11 solutions as well.
Margins to remain pressured: Overall margins were pressured by the PC segment,

driving a sequential decline in 3Q owing to lower PC volumes and utilization. This
margin headwind should intensify in 4Q because of shrinking PC business scale.
Conversely, server gross margin should remain resilient in the high single digits to
10%,  staying flat q/q.
4Q preview: We estimate that 4Q revenue will grow ~8% q/q, to NT$263bn (+38%
y/y), primarily driven by higher PC ASPs despite declining volumes. GM should
contracting ~10bps q/q, to 4.3% (-150bps y/y), with declining PC profitability, mainly

owing to diminishing scale benefits. This leads to our OP estimate of NT$2.8bn
(+25% q/q, +6% y/y) and NP estimate of NT$1.7bn (-34% q/q, +21% y/y), for EPS of
NT$0.39. Our 4Q net profit estimate is down q/q because of lower non-op income.
Stay UW with price target unchanged at NT$23.0 (10.4x CY27 P/E). We think
valuation looks quite stretched now at ~14x CY27e P/E. We prefer other ODMs like
Wistron (6x), Hon Hai (12x), Wiwynn (8x), or Quanta (9x).

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.
Request your ballot                          here.

3Q26 Results Summary
3Q26 gross margin miss:
• Revenue was NT$243bn (+2% q/q, +30% y/y), and GM was 4.4% (-30bps q/q, -
130bps y/y), 80bps and 40bps below our estimate and the Street, respectively,
driven by lower PC margins.
• As a result, OP was NT$2.bn (-21% q/q, -16% y/y), 33% below our estimate/34%
be;low the Street, for OpM of 0.9% (-30bps q/q, -50bps y/y).
• Non-op profit was higher than expected, so net profit reached Nt$2.6bn (-18% q/q,

+33% y/y), 15% above our estimate/2% above the Street, for EPS of NT$0.59.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target is unchanged at  NT$23.0: Our price target is our base case
scenario value, derived from a multistage residual income (RI) model. We use the
company's beginning equity plus the present value of all expected residual income in
excess of the cost of capital. The RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and
negative when it is below.
We assume a cost of equity of 9.1%  (beta of 1.3, equity risk premium of 6.0% and risk-free

rate of 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3% (all
unchanged).
Our new price target implies a 2026e P/E of 10.4x and a 2027e P/E of 8.5x, below the five-
year average of 13.8x. We view these as fair, because we factor in more conservative
assumptions for its consumer business and a weaker margin outlook for Compal. The
company's venture into the "new businesses" (e.g., auto, server, etc.) is constraining its
margin profile as it tries to shift from 80%+ PC to more commercial exposure.

Our bull and bear case values are also unchanged, at NT$38.0 and NT$17.0,
respectively.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment

1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), a 7.0% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.

n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand

n Weaker-than-expected NB demand

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Lotes Co. Ltd  | Asia Pacific
3Q GM Decline Eases; 4Q
Recovery in Focus
3Q results:
• GM came in at 44.8% (-60bps q/q, -640bps y/y), 20bps above our estimate.
3Q GM continued to face margin headwinds from rising raw material costs,
which we highlighted in our recent upgrade report here. We look forward to
management's comment during its results call to see if 4Q margin will be
guided to return to growth.
• Opex was  higher than expected at NT$2.2bn, 10% above our estimate and

9% above the Street.
• So OpM wa only 22.9% (+100bps q/q, -700bps y/y), 180bps below our
estimate and 250bps below the Street.
• Net profit was NT$1,919mn (-3% q/q, -17% y/y) for EPS of NT$17.09, 12%
below our estimate and 12% below the Street.
• Lotes is holding an earnings call on November 13 at 4 PM HKT; we will
provide takeaways after the call.

Key Takeaways
3Q GM fell 60bps q/q, to 44.8%, 20bps above our estimate but 70bps below the
Street. The sequential decline continued to narrow.

EPS of NT$17.09 missed our estimate and the Street by 12%, with opex 10%
above our estimate/9% above the Street.
We believe the new server CPU socket production ramp in 4Q can support our
view of 4Q GM recovery.
The call tomorrow should help clarify that and the opex overshoot.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW)
Primary - Residual Income
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.6% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected growth in the server business, such as from cloud and enter-
prise in China

n Stronger-than-expected DT demand
n Sooner-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n Faster ramp-up of its EV business
Risks to Downside
n Further deceleration of server demand from US hyperscalers
n Weaker-than-expected DT demand
n Slower-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

投資評等 目標價

前次投資建議：2026.5.13： 
「逢低買進」，目標價: $3,300

報告日期：2026.11.16 研究員：陳泂暉

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/13 收盤價： 1,520.00 毛利重返成長軌道，上調投資評等至「買進」 
目前股價 12X27(F)PER，考量： 
(1) 26Q3 公 司 營 收 為 278.54 億元，QoQ+19.6% 、
YoY+51.3%，受惠 HPC 電源需求、SPE 沉積蝕刻及次
系統提升與電氣化推進；毛利率為 32.6% ，
QoQ+1.9ppts、YoY-0.7ppts，費用率創低帶動營益率創
高；稅後淨利 42.78億元，QoQ+43.5%、YoY+62.8%；
稅後 EPS 21.93元。 
(2) 工業業務受惠工廠自動化回溫及能源、電動車等電氣化
需求推進，機器人、航太與醫療等客製化方案亦逐步貢
獻。半導體設備受惠沉積與蝕刻滲透率提升，客戶投資
看至 2030年後，帶動子系統整合與機箱組裝委外需求擴
大。 
(3) HPC受惠 AI基礎設施升級，機櫃內銅纜與機櫃外光纖將
長期共存。公司透過 XFS 強化光學封裝與量產，推進
CPO、12.8T XPO 及 Micro-VCSEL 技術；電源延伸至
Busbars、匯流排槽及水冷板等全液冷機櫃方案，結合
Interplex 機構整合並共同開發 LVDC/HVDC 架構，推升
系統級競爭力。 
(4) 預估 2027 年營收 1,636.24 億元，YoY+54.4%，受惠於
新世代平台放量；毛利率 33.6%，YoY+2.1ppts，受惠於
產品組合改善；稅後淨利 256.69 億元，YoY+74.6%；稅
後 EPS 131.58元。

貿聯目前股價位於 12X27(F)PER，位於公司過往 9-34X區間
下緣位置，考量公司持續推進光通業務，將有助於評價回升，
所以取 17X，給予「買進」投資評等， 6 個月目標價
2,200(17X2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 44.74%。

訪談重點

全球連接線束解決方案領導者 
貿聯成立於 1996 年，總部位於加州矽谷，註冊於開曼群島，為全球連接線
束解決方案的領導廠商。產品分為工業用、電器用、車用、資訊科技與其他
五大產線。主要應用於資訊科技用線、電腦週邊設備、數據中心用線、消費
電子用線、電器用線、車用線、醫療設備用線、光通訊設備、太陽能連接裝
置、工業設備用線、半導體設備等線材、連接器等領域。公司 26Q1 產品營
收比重為: 工業用 31%、電器用 12%、車用 8%、資訊科技 48%。

HPC 與半導體設備雙引擎驅動營益率創歷史新高 
26Q3 公司營收為 278.54 億元，QoQ+19.6%、YoY+51.3%，細分產品組合
來看，HPC 高效能運算受惠於 AI 基礎設施持續投資，其中電源互連因高電
流與機櫃級配電需求強勁、旗下 XFS光學業務月營收運行率自年初以來已超
過翻倍、SPE半導體生產設備受惠於沉積與蝕刻等設備平台滲透率及次系統
承接比重提升，並持續大幅優於整體 WFE 市場、其他業務整體市場環境好
壞參半，但電氣化與自動化需求持續推進，另併購之 Interplex於 26Q3 僅計
入 7天營收，10月起完整併表並單月貢獻 6,700萬美元；毛利率為 32.6%，
QoQ+1.9ppts、YoY-0.7ppts，搭配營業費用率降至歷史新低，帶動營業利益
率創下歷史新高；稅後淨利 42.78 億元，QoQ+43.5%、YoY+62.8%；稅後
EPS 21.93元。

動能從單一零組件邁向子系統級解決方案，動能延續至 2027年後 
工業用業務方面，受惠工廠自動化需求活動持續改善，加上受惠於電氣化與
基礎設施現代化的能源、電動車及特用工程應用穩健推進，帶動整體表現持
續成長；此外，公司在機器人、航太、海事、醫療科技、半導體量測及儲能
等領域的客製化線束與整合方案，部分已成為穩定貢獻來源，部分則處於早
期認證與商業開發階段。

半導體生產設備方面，主要受惠於沉積與蝕刻設備平台滲透率與內含價值提
升，且客戶針對高複雜度製程的投資預期強勁並延伸至 2030 年以後，帶動
高複雜度組裝、子系統製造與整合、測試及機箱組裝的委外需求持續擴大。
隨著各產業客戶對供應商的要求從單一零組件轉向具備工程支援、產品認

訪談速報 
證、量產準備度與穩定執行力的子系統級解決方案，結合併購 Interplex所帶
來的精密機構與機殼整合綜效，相關成長動能預期將延續至 2027 年及更長
遠的基礎設施與產能擴張週期。

機櫃內銅纜、機櫃外光纖並進，提供完整互連方案 
公司持續受惠於高速銅互連解決方案在支援更高頻寬與維持訊號完整性上的
優勢；隨著 AI系統頻寬需求、光纖密度與互連複雜度提高，光互連基礎設施
的重要性持續提升，同時銅互連在機櫃內短距離傳輸、成本與部署特性上仍
具效率優勢，因此光互連不會單向取代銅互連，而是機櫃內以銅纜為主、機
櫃外長距離傳輸以光纖為主，兩者將依頻寬、傳輸距離、功耗與封裝架構不
同而長期共存。公司透過旗下 XFS Communications 補強精密光學組裝、連
接器化製程、高密度封裝與快速量產擴張能力，並陸續於 OFC、Computex
與 OCP展出光纖方案、CPO交換器托盤及 12.8T XPO收發方案，同時推進
次世代基於 Micro-VCSEL的機櫃內擴展（Scale-up）光學互連技術與外部技
術合作，成為後續 HPC業務擴張的重要動能。

在 HPC端，AI基礎設施進入長期投資與升級週期，隨著算力密度持續攀升，
資料中心的物理基礎設施挑戰正延伸至高電流電力傳輸、散熱及系統級整
合。公司產品線已從單一電源線延伸至 Busbars、Power Whips、連接器、
匯流排槽以及水冷板、分歧管、液冷快接頭與漏液偵測等全液冷 AI 機櫃方
案，並結合併購 Interplex的精密機構與機殼整合能力強化系統級競爭力；至
於低壓直流與高壓直流新電源架構的導入管理層指出將取決於各家客戶的系
統設計、產品認證進度及資料中心電力基礎設施的就緒程度，目前公司正與
客戶密切共同開發次世代與下下世代架構，以高度彈性配合其部署排程。

新世代平台放量帶動獲利爆發 
預估 26Q4營收 339.62億元，QoQ+21.9%、YoY+71.7%，因為工業用季節
性需求回溫，以及 HPC 新平台所帶動；毛利率 32.8%，QoQ+0.2ppts、
YoY+0.9ppts，因新平台導入，因此壓抑毛利率成長幅度；稅後淨利 51.7億
元，QoQ+20.9%、YoY+88.7%；稅後 EPS 26.5元。

預估 2026年營收 1,059.61億元，YoY+48.7%，持續受惠於資料中心業務；
毛利率 31.5%，YoY-0.2ppts；稅後淨利 147.04 億元，YoY+63.3%；稅後
EPS 75.37元。

預估 2027年營收 1,636.24 億元，YoY+54.4%，受惠於新世代平台放量；毛
利率 33.6%，YoY+2.1ppts，受惠於產品組合改善；稅後淨利 256.69 億元，
YoY+74.6%；稅後 EPS 131.58元。

張智皓 chihhao,chang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

虹堡 (5258 TT) 
2027 年營運重回成長

永豐觀點 
目前訂單能見度約一季，較先前有所改善。美洲客戶需求持續看增，預估明年成長
10%-20%。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係考量(1) 新客戶訂單將逐漸貢獻營收，並持續精簡費用，
提升管理效率，2027 年獲利成長可期。(2) 主要營收區域歐洲及北美地區訂單狀
況皆轉好。評價上，以 2027 年稅後 EPS 5.04 元計算，目前本益比約 8 倍，明顯
低於過去五年本益比區間 10-17 倍，考量營運將重返成長，故給予 10 倍本益比
(原 11 倍)，維持買進的投資建議，下修目標價至 50 元。 
 近八季營收及 YoY 趨勢圖

### Page 2

2

全球前 15 大 POS 終端機製造業者：虹堡成立於 1993 年總部位於新北市新店區，
主要從事 POS 設備及相關產品之設計、開發與銷售，為台灣電子金融交易終端機
(POS)及 IC 晶片卡讀卡機製造商。主要營業項目為電子金融交易終端機(POS)及相
關產品之設計、開 發、銷售，這類終端機通過國際組織認證後經客戶在全球各地
通過當地收單機構驗證後即可用做當地商家收單使用。產品目前可支援各類型的支
付方式，包含非接觸卡片(contactless card)、晶片卡(chip card)、磁條卡(magnetic 
strip card)及新興的二維條碼及指紋辨識支付等。虹堡產品可概分為電子金融交易
終端機、個人金融應用產品、電子式收銀機及週邊設備等。國內主要競爭對手：振
樺電、樺漢、飛捷、伍豐等。 
記憶體價格飆漲，3Q26 獲利低於預期：在歐洲地區需求略有回穩下，虹堡 3Q26
營收 24.25 億元(+3.3%QoQ，+18.4%YoY)，受到記憶體價格上漲影響，毛利率
31.5%(+3pts QoQ，+4.6pts YoY)，因費用控管得宜，營利率 9.9%(+3.1pts QoQ，
+3.1pts YoY)，稅後淨利 1.5 億元(+25%QoQ，+8.1%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 1.36 元，
低於預期的 1.52 元。 
物料狀況預期轉好，4Q26 持續成長：虹堡 10 月營收 6.83 億元(-27.4%MoM，-
1.8%YoY)，衰退係受原物料價格上漲及法國動亂影響，持累積第四季達成率
27.1%，低於預期，然管理層預期上下半年營收比重 45:55，預估在零組件價格穩
定後，訂單將穩定出貨。研究處預估 4Q26 營收 25.16 億元(+3.4%QoQ，
+8.6%YoY)，考量零組件漲價未能為轉轉嫁給客戶，毛利率 31.8%(+0.3pts QoQ，
-3.5pts YoY)，營利率 10%(+0.1pts QoQ，-2.7pts YoY)，稅後淨利 2.12 億元
(+41.6%QoQ，-27.5%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 1.92 元。2026 年受記憶體價格上漲，
客戶下單轉為保守，預估 2026 年營收 89.1 億元(+13.3%YoY)，毛利率 30.9%(-
4pts YoY)，營業淨利 6.29 億元(-1%YoY)，稅後淨利 4.15 億元(-10.6%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 為 3.76 元。 
美洲地區客戶將開花結果，2027 年獲利重返成長：展望 2027 年，若以地區區分，
虹堡北美及南美客戶進展順利，許多專案已獲得客戶認證將開始出貨；歐洲地區，
法國新銀行訂單將持續出貨；亞太地區，因日本基期已高，2027 年預估微幅成長。
整體來說， 2027 年營收及獲利將重回成長的一年，然需關注記憶體價格對獲利及
訂單影響。研究部預估 2027 年營收 90.5 億元(+1.6%YoY)，毛利率 31.5%(+0.6pts 
YoY)，營業淨利 8.21 億元(+30.6%YoY)，稅後淨利 5.56 億元(+33.9%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 為 5.04 元。

公司研究報告

將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
1 
6415 矽力杰( Silergy Corp.)

報告日期：2026/11/16          研究員：王蕎程(Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 本業獲利持續改善：3Q26 營業利益季增 29.7%，營益率提升
至 13.26%；惟 2Q26 受金融資產評價利益挹注，墊高比較基
期，第三季獲利下滑主要來自業外收益回落，並非本業衰退。 
 AIDC 與車用雙引擎帶動成長：AIDC 營收年增逾 120%，新一
代 SPS 預計 2027 年開始放量；車用受惠 MCU 及高壓大電流
產品導入，帶動單車價值提升，預期 2027 年兩大業務營收均
有望成長逾 50%。 
 製程升級與供貨優勢支撐成長：Gen4 產品持續放量，預期 2027
年底營收占比達 50%，搭 配 12 吋產能布局及 Gen5 新品量產，
有利爭取大型客戶，惟封測產能吃緊及成本上升仍為毛利改善
的觀察重點。

一、3Q26 本業獲利持續改善，預估 4Q26 EPS 2.5 元 
矽力*-KY 為電源管理 IC 設計廠，3Q26 營收 72.59 億元（QoQ 
+20.9%、YoY +52.4%），主要受惠 AIDC、工業及車用需求成長，
以及消費電子客戶提前拉貨。毛利率 47.40%，QoQ +0.4 個百分點，
主要因產品組合改善；營業利益 9.63 億元（QoQ +29.7%），營業
利益率 13.26%，QoQ +0.88 個百分點，本業獲利持續改善。惟 2Q26
受金融資產評價利益 9.59 億元挹注，墊高獲利基期，3Q26 因業外
損益回落至 1.11 億元，稅後淨利降至 9.09 億元（QoQ -42.1%），
EPS 2.34 元，獲利下滑主要來自業外收益減少，並非本業衰退。
展望 4Q26，消費電子需求受第三季提前拉貨及終端市場疲弱影
響，手機、電視等產品出貨轉弱，惟資料中心需求仍維持成長，
預估營收 74.63 億元，毛利率 47.80%，稅後 EPS 2.50 元。

二、產品組合優化，AIDC 與車用為明年主要成長動能 
AIDC 為公司主要成長來源，3Q26 營收占比達 17%，YoY +120%。
隨 AI 伺服器 CPU 及 GPU 功耗提高，主板供電系統需要處理更大
電流，同時降低電力損耗及散熱負擔，帶動高效率電源管理 IC 需
求。其中，新一代 SPS（Smart Power Stage）將功率元件、驅動器
及感測功能整合於單一封裝，可改善高電流供電效率並縮小電路
板空間，目前已進入多家客戶驗證階段，預期 2027 年開始貢獻營
收。公司表示，北美客戶對相關產品的需求較為迫切，預期放量
速度將快於中國客戶；此外，伺服器主板、資料中心通訊設備及
SSD 為 AIDC 主要成長應用，三者合計可占 AI 相關營收約 70%～

三、車用單車價值提升，光通訊與 12 吋製程布局 
車用方面，公司持續增加單一車款的晶片供應種類，除既有電源
管理產品外，高壓、大電流電源 IC、MCU 及電池管理相關產品陸
續導入域控制器、電驅等應用，帶動單車產品價值提升。公司表
示，高階車款單車產品價值預計由 2026 年底的 200～220 美元，提
升至 2027 年的 270～300 美元；海外新客戶近期已開始小批量出
貨，明年貢獻可望增加，惟中國客戶仍為主要營收來源，預期 2027
年車用營收成長逾 50%。光通訊方面，隨 AI 資料中心傳輸速率提
升，光模組對供電效率、功耗及散熱的要求同步提高，帶動高效
率電源轉換、雷射驅動電源及整合式電源管理產品需求。公司已
布局 NPO 及 CPO 相關產品，並進入客戶設計導入階段。

製程方面，公司布局 12 吋晶圓產能，目前的產能可支應至少 20
億美元營收規模。隨 AIDC 及車用需求增加，類比 IC 供應鏈產能
趨緊，部分同業仍以 8 吋製程為主，矽力則因 12 吋產能準備較為
充足，有利爭取重視供貨穩定性的大型客戶。公司表示，Gen4 產
品持續放量，預期 2027 年底營收占比約 50%，Gen5 則預計於 2027
年量產。新製程除改善成本結構外，也能支援 AIDC 對電源轉換效
率、功率密度及產品整合度的要求，有利提升高階產品比重。

供應鏈方面，目前主要瓶頸在封裝測試，受設備供應不足及裝機
速度限制，產品生產週期由原先約 4 個月延長至 9 個月，公司須
提前備妥晶圓等待後段排產，帶動在製品增加。預期 2027 年上半
年封測產能仍難明顯改善，較大幅度的產能紓解可能落在 2027 年
下半年至 2028 年。雖然公司前段晶圓供應相對充足，但後段產能
限制仍可能影響出貨速度，且成本上升未必能完全轉嫁客戶。

四、預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 10.87 / 14.72 元 
預估 2026 年營收 255.85 億元（YoY +36.0%），毛利率 47.58%，營
業利益 31.33 億元，稅後 EPS 10.87 元。展望 2027 年，受惠 AIDC
及車用新品放量、Gen4 良率改善與高階產品比重提升，預估營收
319.81 億元（YoY +25.0%），毛利率提升至 50.07%；營業利益 57.83
億元（YoY +84.6%）。考量業外收益回歸正常水準，預估稅後 EPS 
14.72 元（YoY +35.4%）。 
五、投資建議「買進」，目標價 600 元 
綜合考量：(1) AIDC 需求持續成長，帶動伺服器主板、SSD 及光

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一. 公司簡介 
矽力杰股份有限公司（簡稱：矽力 KY；代號：6415）主要從事類
比、數位混和 IC 之研發設計及銷售，產品應用範圍廣泛，主要
應用於 3C 產業、雲端伺服器、工業類、汽車、5G 通訊等相關
領域，包括 LED 照明、平板電腦、筆記型電腦、固態硬碟、視
頻監控、伺服器、數位機上盒、智能 音箱、智慧型手機、多媒體
電視、LED 背光模組、路由器、行動電源、汽車電子、通訊基地
台及智能電錶等，並持續增加各種終端應用，如 TV Panel、手機
快充、醫療領域等。

（資料來源：矽力 KY 2025 年報）

矽力 KY 目前產品主要是類比 IC 晶片，產品廣泛應用於消費性
電子產品、工業用產品、資訊產品、5G 網路通訊產品及汽車電子
產品。針對終端產品設計需求，該公司可提供不同的晶片，例如
平板電腦、電池充電管理晶片、直流對直流轉換晶片、過流保護
晶片、靜電防護裝置、LED 背光驅動晶片以及 PMU 等；針對 LED 
照明，該公司可提供 LED 照明驅動晶片；針對機上盒，該公司
可提供交流對直流轉換晶片，直流對直流轉換晶片，過流保護晶
片，EMI 防護裝置等；智慧電表，則提供包含能源監控管理(Energy 
measurement IC)的系統晶片 System on Chip (SOC) 以及高精度電
流檢測方案。

二. 產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
(1)半導體市場概況與 IC 設計業 
半導體產業為工業類設備、資訊電腦、網路通訊、消費性及汽車
電子產品應用的關鍵性產業，亦是目前主導性的基礎產業之一，
隨著 3C(電腦 Computer、通訊 Communication、消費 Consumer)產
品的蓬勃發展和工業類產品智慧化和低能耗的需求，產品生命週
期縮短而更加快速，並朝多元化及豐富化發展，隨著產品推陳出
新與成長，均促進未來半導體需求及市場穩定發展。國內半導體
產業鏈發展日益成熟，有完整的上、中、下游分工體系，使得 IC 
設計公司得以專精於從事設計工作，再將 IC 的製造委託給晶圓
代工製作成晶圓半成品，經過前段測試後再交由封裝廠進行切割
及封裝，最後再經專業測試廠做後段測 試後，完成最後產品，也
造就了良好的 IC 設計產業發展環境。 IC 元件一般可分為數位
邏輯 IC 及類比 IC 兩大類。數位 IC 利用 0、1 非連續性方式
傳遞訊號，多用於處理數位資訊如計算、控制、儲存等，強調尺
寸、速度與功耗(Process Driven)。類比 IC 則負責處理來自自然界
的連續性類比訊號，如光、 熱、電、速度、壓力等物理量，並作
為橋接上述各類物理訊息與數位電子系統的媒介，需同時藉由製
程、電路設計和半導體元件物理的相互配合，在晶片效能及成本 
上尋求最適化，由於其決定了數位產品所能呈現的最終呈現品

### Page 5

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
5 
質，因此更為注重元件的特性(Design Driven)，琢磨於可靠度、穩
定度、功率消耗、能源轉換效率、電壓電流控制能力等

(2)電源管理 IC 
隨著環保與節能議題發酵，節能科技為目前重要發展課題之一，
而類比 IC 中 的電源管理 IC 正扮演著不可或缺的關鍵性角
色，電源管理 IC 主要作為在不同的系統運作狀態下，隨時控制
並保持系統內適當電流與電壓正常供給。而電源供應器的好壞，
將會直接影響電子產品的使用壽命。近年來電源供應器均朝向輕
薄短小、省電、耐用的方向發展。而要產品達到這些目標的重要
關鍵，乃在於如何把電源供應器做到高效率(高效能)、高穩壓率和
低雜訊干擾，這其中所有參與管制功能的 IC 即被稱為「電源控
制、管理類比 IC」，每年市場均保持穩定成長，消費電子、5G 網
路通信、移動互聯領域、雲端伺服器、工業應用都是目前最主要
市場，未來並朝向人工智慧及電動車及車連網方向發展。電源管
理單元(PMU)是一種高度集成的電源管理方案，將傳統分立的若干
類電源管理器件整合在單個的封裝之內，可實現更高的電源轉換
效率、更低功耗，以及更少的元件數。PMU 在有限的體積內集成
了盡可能多的電源通道，根據各個子單元的需求、提供高效率的
供電，或提供低雜訊的乾淨電源，又或者是系統必須的上電時序
控制，成為為系統供電的首選。過去十年智慧型手持式裝置在市
場快速發展，總體來看，終端市場整體還將保持快速成長，在此
大背景下，PMU 的市場需求有望實現較之前市場預期更高的發展
態勢。隨著智慧型手持式產品的大螢幕化、輕薄化，電池的體積
也將會越來越小，在相應的電池容量不變的情況下，要求整機的
續航能力要更強，除了不斷降低晶片功耗改善以外，對電源的智
慧化管理也變得不可或缺，因此未來 PMU 市場還會有不錯發展。

未來展望 
電源管理 IC 是類比 IC 最基礎和重要的產品，近年來電源管理
一直是半導體領域的熱門市場之一，且電源管理 IC 是所有電子
產品必備的關鍵零組件，屬於高功率類比電路技術，主要用途為
提供電子產品穩定且適當電壓的電源，將直接影響電子產品的使
用壽命。隨著全球 3C 產品的功能不斷增加，汽車自動駕駛和工
業自動 化需求，終端應用漸漸走向低耗電、輕薄短小與多功能整
合以及對產品壽命與可靠度要求更高的趨勢，為達到此目的，電
源管理 IC 必須做到使電源系統效率提升、節能及縮小 IC 元件
封裝以減少所佔用之電路板面積等重點。另隨著 CPU 不斷向上 
躍進，對電源的穩定性及電壓的精準度也就更為要求。此外，高
整合度不但能減少零件數量，達到降低系統耗電以及提升系統可
靠度及品質等效益，並可提高生產良率，且降低生產工時，進而

6 
降低成本。而隨著環保與節能的議題發酵，節能科技為目前重要
發展課題之一，電源管理 IC 扮演不可或缺的關鍵角色。除低耗
電、極低的靜態消耗電流等需求外，低耗電使用無鉛封裝製程亦
是全球各國對環保、低耗能等綠色概念的要求重點，而電源管理 
IC 同時也得考量未來環保法規帶來的限制，故符合環保及綠色能
源將成為電源管理 IC 設計的重要方向。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一、預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 2.50 元 
矽力*-KY 3Q26 營收 72.59 億元（QoQ +20.9%、YoY +52.4%），受
惠 AIDC、工業及車用需求成長，毛利率 47.40%，營業利益 9.63
億元（QoQ +29.7%）；惟 2Q26 金融資產評價利益墊高比較基期，
3Q26 業外收益回落，稅後淨利 9.09 億元（QoQ -42.1%），EPS 2.34
元。展望 4Q26，消費電子需求轉弱，惟資料中心需求維持成長，
預估營收 74.63 億元（QoQ +2.8%），毛利率 47.80%，稅後淨利 9.76
億元，EPS 2.50 元。

二、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.87 元 
預估 2026 年營收 255.85 億元（YoY +36.0%），受惠 AIDC 及車用
需求增加，毛利率 47.58%，營業毛利 121.74 億元；營業利益 31.33
億元，營業利益率 12.25%，業外損益 15.70 億元，稅後淨利 42.24
億元，稅後 EPS 10.87 元。

三、預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.72 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠 AIDC 新一代 SPS 產品導入、車用單車價值提
升，以及 Gen4 製程良率改善與高階產品比重增加，預估全年營收
319.81 億元（YoY +25.0%），營業毛利 160.12 億元，毛利率 50.07%；
營業利益 57.83 億元（YoY +84.6%），營業利益率 18.08%，業外
損益 4.51 億元，稅後淨利 57.34 億元（YoY +35.7%），稅後 EPS 14.72
元。

## Document 214: [000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.11.02  
잠시 쉬어갑니다

3Q26 Review 
기아는 3Q26P 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.4% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회했다. 영업이익 증
감 요인은 환율 효과(+5,290억원), 물량 효과(+4,420억원), 믹스 효과(-4,650억원), 
인센티브/가격 효과(-7,270억원), 재료비 영향(-3,680억원), 기타(+4,130억원)이다. 
판매 성장에도 높아진 수익성 부담 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.9% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.1만대
(+8.8%)를 기록했다. 미국 HEV와 서유럽 EV 판매가 각각 118%, 163% 증가하며 
글로벌 점유율은 4.0%까지 확대됐다. 다만, 판매 성장에도 원화 강세와 원자재 가격 
상승, EV 비중 확대에 따른 믹스 부담으로 수익성은 전분기 대비 하락했다. 유럽 내 
EV 판매가 늘어나면서 믹스가 악화되었으며, 전쟁으로 인한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 
재료비 부담은 전년 대비 약 3,700억 증가했다.  
연간 판매목표 335만대는 유지했으나, 4분기 수익성의 급격한 반등은 어려울 전망이
다. 향후 미국 텔루라이드·스포티지 HEV 공급 확대와 유럽 EV 판매 증가에 따른 고정
비 흡수가 수익성 회복의 핵심이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 155,000원으로 하향 
기아에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하고, 실적 추정치 하향을 반영해 목표주가를 
155,000원으로 하향한다. 재료비 부담, 환율 효과 축소, 예상보다 큰 믹스 악화로 단
기 이익 눈높이 조정은 불가피하다. 다만 산업수요 둔화에도 주요 시장에서 점유율을 
확대되고 있다는 점은 긍정적이다. 현재 배당 수익률은 7% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높
은 배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

## Document 230: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%)과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%)을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

## Document 275: Anthropic上市次日暴跌，盘中一度离破发仅差5美分，AI交易“买方罢工”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic上市次日暴跌，盘中一度离破发仅差5美分，AI交易“买方罢工”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 股价周五盘中⼀度⼤跌 15% ⾄ 83.05 美元，距 83 美元的发⾏价仅剩 5 美分，最终
收跌 8.5% 。市场⼈⼠描述当前局⾯为 AI 交易的买⽅罢⼯。投资者观望情绪浓厚， Vega IS
表⽰，若股价接近甚⾄跌破发⾏价，将考虑在公开市场买⼊。 Tikehau Capital 表⽰，希望
等待 IPO 波动性消退后再考虑进⼀步介⼊。
摘要：
史上最⼤ IPO 的光环正在消退。 Anthropic 上市第⼆⽇股价急剧下滑，⼏乎抹去⾸⽇涨幅，映射
出更⼴泛 AI 与科技板块的抛售情绪，同时也为竞争对⼿ OpenAI 的未来上市之路埋下变数。
Anthropic 股价周五盘中⼀度⼤跌 15% ⾄ 83.05 美元，距 83 美元的发⾏价仅剩 5 美分，随后⼩幅
反弹，最终收跌 8.5% 。该股在上市⾸⽇曾短暂将公司市值推升⾄⾼于亚⻢逊。
与此同时，承销银⾏⾏使超额配售权（绿鞋机制），进⼀步扩⼤股票供给，使 Anthropic 此次 IPO
及联合私募合计融资规模升⾄ 1195 亿美元。

市场⼤环境同样构成压⼒。周五，抛售浪潮从亚洲蔓延⾄美国，纳斯达克 100 指数下跌 1.5% ，
标普 500 指数跌 0.5% ，芝加哥期权交易所波动率指数（ VIX ）飙升⾄ 29 以上 —— 远超企业通常视
为市场欢迎 IPO 的⽔位线。
Anthropic 此次上市的战略意义超出单⼀公司范畴。作为⾸家上市的前沿 AI 模型开发商，其市场
表现实质上是对投资者能否为 AI 底层技术本⾝（⽽⾮芯⽚、数据中⼼等基础设施）定价买单的⼀
次直接测试。
买盘观望， AI 热度遭遇 " 买⽅罢⼯ "
Forte Securities 的 Emmanuel Valavanis 将当前局⾯描述为 " 针对 Anthropic 乃⾄整个 AI 和科技
交易的买⽅罢⼯ " 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
他指出，许多客⼾在此次 IPO 配售中未能获得份额，在未持有仓位、也⽆指数跟踪压⼒的情况
下，这部分投资者宁愿在场外等待更⻓期的价格发现，也不愿贸然接盘。
这⼀动态揭⽰出 IPO ⾸⽇溢价背后的脆弱性：当核⼼买家已在发⾏价锁定筹码，⼆级市场缺乏增
量资⾦⼊场时，股价承压⼏乎是必然结果。
对于投资者⽽⾔，当前市场波动加剧了观望情绪。Vega IS 基⾦经理 Olivier David 表⽰，若股价
接近甚⾄跌破发⾏价，其团队将考虑在公开市场买⼊。
历史性 IPO ：融资规模与市场地位
此次上市创下多项纪录。 Anthropic 成为史上最⼤规模 IPO 的主⻆，同时也是⾸家登陆美股的前
沿 AI 模型开发商，超越了 SpaceX 今年 6 ⽉创下的 866 亿美元纪录。
在承销层⾯，⾼盛、摩根⼠丹利及另外 20 家银⾏共计获得 5.5 亿美元费⽤，同样刷新历史纪录。

IPO 及联合私募合计融资中， IPO 部分为 935 亿美元，私募部分为 260 亿美元。按当前股价计算，
Anthropic 市值约为 1.5 万亿美元，位列全球上市公司前 15 位。公司七位联合创始⼈的合计财富升
⾄ 1660 亿美元。
包括 Anthropic 在内，美国市场今年⾸次公开募股总规模已达 2580 亿美元，超越 2021 年 1950 亿
美元的历史⾼点（均剔除特殊⽬的收购公司等⾦融⼯具）。
基本⾯⽀撑：企业客⼾驱动营收⾼速增⻓
股价波动之外， Anthropic 的基本⾯数据提供了部分⽀撑。
根据监管⽂件，截⾄ 10 ⽉ 31 ⽇，公司年化运营收⼊达 810 亿美元，较 9 ⽉底增加 100 亿美元。截
⾄ 9 ⽉底，年度贡献收⼊超过 10 万美元的企业客⼾数量超过 11900 家，⽽ 2025 年初这⼀数字仅约
为 1500 家。
公司旗下⾯向软件开发者的编程代理产品 Claude Code 在业内获得⼴泛应⽤，监管⽂件显⽰其⽤
⼾甚⾄涵盖 NASA ⼯程师。 Anthropic 以专注企业客⼾为核⼼策略，这⼀路径赢得了部分机构投

资者的认可。
Tikehau Capital 资本市场策略主管 Raphael Thuin 对此评价称， " 这家公司在短短数年内创造的财
富令⼈印象深刻，⾃由现⾦流消耗也相对合理 " ，但同时表⽰，其团队在 AI 领域更倾向于布局多
元化业务，且希望等待 IPO 后的波动性消退后再考虑进⼀步介⼊。

## Document 328: FICC日报：宏观情绪再次下探，关注APEC会议

Source PDF: all report pdf/FICC日报：宏观情绪再次下探，关注APEC会议.pdf
Report source: 华泰期货
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

FICC日报 | 2026-11-17

宏观情绪再次下探，关注APEC会议 
市场分析 
关注美联储加息计价上升可能。美国10月非农数据大超预期，新增16.5万人，且薪资增速超预期，美国10月核心
CPI环比上涨0.3%，高于市场预期的0.2%，整体CPI环比上涨0.4%，同样超出预期的0.3%。经济韧性的背景叠加油
价短期居高不下，通胀向其他领域传导或将更顺畅，中期选举后多为美联储票委发表鹰派讲话，需要警惕加息计
价上升的可能。此外，截至11月9日，10Y法德利差录得1.476%，较峰值下降13BP，高于10Y意德利差的1.04%，短
期宏观风险略有缓和。 
中东局势再度反复。随着共和党在中期选举上失利，美国进一步推进美伊和谈的意愿上升，但若伊朗方要价过高
和谈也较难推进，此前美伊双方谈判重点有所分歧，美方聚焦伊朗去核化，伊朗聚焦美国撤军。11月8日美国总统
特朗普在接受媒体采访时明确表示不打算升级与伊朗的战事，同时美方媒体还透露万斯过去两周积极推动新停火
并试图恢复核谈。11月11日，伊朗最高国家安全委员会秘书雷扎伊表示，伊朗不会让一点浓缩铀运出境。11月12
日，特朗普表示“不需要与伊朗达成协议 达成协议的时代早已过去”。随着特朗普的最新表态，美伊局势朝着长期
僵持局势发展的概率正持续上升。 
国内稳信心政策陆续出台。按美元计，中国10月出口同比增长41.6%，进口同比增35.2%，均高于预期。10月投资、
消费数据表现偏弱。10月20日发改委新闻发布会释放正在研究制定扩大内需战略实施方案，11月2日央行推出超
1000亿证券、基金、保险公司互换便利(SFISF)推动市场信心企稳。关注11月17-18日的APEC会议。 
分板块来看，关注农产品的机会。民主党全面掌控国会压制铜关税预期以及油价，短期利空铜价、利好黄金，远
端则需要关注加息预期的变化。能源方面，尽管美伊局势仍往僵持方向发展，但实际中东的原油出口量明显回升，
在不造成生产设施破坏的前提下，油品及化工将温和调整。农产品则关注厄尔尼诺的气候预期，以及对应减产影
响，10月份USDA报告出现产量不及预期的信号，农产品有望重新启动。

策略 
商品和股指期货：部分农产品逢低做多。

风险 
地缘政治风险（能源板块上行风险）；全球经济超预期下行（风险资产下行风险）；美联储超预期收紧（风险资产
下行风险）；海外流动性风险冲击（风险资产下行风险）。

## Document 340: Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Jan Hatzius 
+1(212)902-0394 | jan.hatzius@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
Global Views: Downside Growth Risks
26 October 2026 | 6:45AM EDT
1. So far in 2026, global growth has been remarkably unremarkable.  Both the world 
as a whole and the G3 economies are growing in line with our estimates of potential 
GDP.  Our interpretation is that fiscal easing and the positive impulse from the AI 
boom have offset the negative impulse from the war in Iran and the lingering drag 
from higher US tariffs.

2. Our standing global GDP forecasts project more of the same in 2027, as lower 
energy prices offset fading tailwinds from fiscal policy and AI.  But the balance of 
risks is on the downside.  The most immediate concern is that, far from subsiding, 
energy prices have again climbed sharply on fears of renewed Middle East escalation.  
The prices of gasoline, diesel, jet fuel, and European/Asian natural gas are now near 
or even above their March/April highs, with likely negative effects on consumers and 
energy-importing economies more broadly.

### Page 2

3. Although we don’t expect significant fiscal policy changes in 2027, the consequences 
of large-scale deficits—coupled with AI-linked borrowing and central bank hikes—have 
started to show up more clearly in higher longer-term interest rates.  The moves have 
been mostly orderly, but the increase since early 2026 averages a sizable 100bp across 
DM yield curves.  If sustained, we estimate that this increase will subtract a cumulative 
0.6% from GDP via weakness in housing, consumer durables, and business investment.  
(Only part of the drag is incorporated in our standing forecasts because we expect bond 
yields to pull back as central banks deliver fewer hikes than priced in markets.)

Exhibit 2: Oil Price Risks Are Skewed to the Upside

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 3

4. The growth impulse from the AI boom was set to peak in late 2026 or early 2027 even 
before concerns around the sustainability of the AI investment boom intensified in 
recent weeks.  Although AI investment is still rising rapidly, we expect the increase as a 
share of GDP to slow over the next year.  Alongside the fading boost from last year’s 
increase in US depreciation allowances, this will weigh on capital spending growth, 
especially in the US.  AI has also boosted US consumer spending growth via a positive 
wealth effect worth an estimated 0.4pp (or 0.25% of GDP) over the past year.  This 
impact is likely to decline over the next year, gradually if stocks perform in line with our 
strategists’ reasonably upbeat expectations but more sharply if AI-related stocks 
continue to decline.

5. Our own base case remains that AI investment will ultimately pay off in terms of higher 
productivity and economywide profits.  However, one key channel for this payoff is labor 
market disruption, which has negative near-term macroeconomic effects.  And while DM 
labor markets still look solid in aggregate, we are starting to see clearer signs that the AI 
impact is spreading beyond the technology sector.  Across nonmanufacturing industries, 
the share of US firms that have adopted AI in regular business functions now shows a 
statistically significant negative correlation with employment growth and a statistically 
significant positive correlation with unemployment.

6. The Fed’s recent signals—explicit from Williams/Jefferson and implicit from Warsh—
effectively rule out a hike on October 28.  But the FOMC still looks very likely to deliver 
another 25bp hike to 4-4.25% on December 9 because spot inflation remains too high.  
And while we continue to expect significant disinflation next year on the back of 
reduced impulses from tariffs, energy prices, and AI-related prices, the risks to our 
forecast are on the upside, most obviously in energy.  Higher inflation could result in

another hike in March, but either way we see renewed cuts in late 2027/early 2028.

7. Like the Fed, the ECB will likely hold rates steady next week and deliver a final hike (to 
2.75%) at the December meeting.  However, the turbulence in the French bond market 
poses somewhat greater downside risks to that call than in the US.  The turbulence has 
tightened financial conditions to a degree that is likely to shave ¼-½pp from annualized 
growth in coming quarters.  And if it intensifies into December, markets might perceive a 
hike as signaling insufficient focus by the ECB on the risk of Euro area fragmentation.  
While the ECB could address this issue by coupling a hike with renewed balance sheet 
expansion, the hurdle for such a move is high, in part because purchases of French 
bonds would inevitably raise concerns about moral hazard with respect to the needed 
fiscal adjustment.

### Page 6

November 5.  Consistent with our view that the UK economy is stronger than widely 
perceived, GDP growth has surprised on the upside this year and the unemployment rate 
declined to 4.7% in August on a 3-month average basis (with the volatile 1-month rate 
plunging to 4.1%).  Rising inflation and the looming household energy price reset in 
January imply a significant risk of further moves, although market pricing of 4+ hikes 
(including November) still looks excessive.  We also expect hikes in Canada and New 
Zealand this week and in Australia next week.

9. Slightly faster than expected growth in Q3 has pushed up our 2026 GDP forecast for 
China to 4.6%.  But most activity indicators are much weaker.  Both property activity and 
fixed asset investment are contracting year-on-year, and while nominal retail sales 
growth picked up to +1.1% in September, large retailers (whose sales are relatively 
better measured) are still posting substantial year-on-year declines.  With domestic 
demand rising slowly at best, sustained GDP growth in the official 4.5-5% target range 
will require ongoing steep increases in the trade surplus.  But the surplus is already at 1% 
of global GDP, and further increases will eat more noticeably into output and 
employment abroad.  Ultimately, China’s trading partners may respond by imposing 
trade barriers that weigh on Chinese exports and GDP, although the measures taken so 
far have been limited.

Exhibit 8: UK Growth Forecasts Have Drifted Higher

Source: Bloomberg, Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   6
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 7

10. Our strongest global view is that central banks will deliver fewer hikes than currently 
priced.  In addition, our rates strategists flag underweight positioning, diminished 
technical risks, and risk asset pressures as reasons why the recent rally might extend.  
The market’s narrow breadth makes our equity strategists wary of momentum factor 
exposure, but they still expect the S&P 500 to resume its climb after the midterm 
election, as earnings continue to grow solidly.  Our credit strategists remain cautious on 
IG technology bonds but see scope for some near-term reversal in HY given the scale of 
the recent selloff.  Our FX strategists expect the Japanese yen to act as a hedge against 
further AI jitters, in part because such jitters would shrink US-Japan interest rate 
differentials.  And our commodity strategists recommend targeted exposures to oil 
products and European natural gas to hedge near-term escalation risks alongside 
longer-term exposures to copper, gold, and select power markets to benefit from 
structural supply/demand imbalances. 
Jan Hatzius

Editorial
Tactical plays within a diversified allocation 
Strategy summary: Our calls to dial down risk since early 
October have helped mitigate a bear market in semiconductors 
and the sharpest single-day post-mid-term election downturn in 
US equities in over three decades. Volatility has eased after the 
post-election spike, helping the IT sector partly recover. We see 
further upside in our opportunistic US IT and healthcare sector 
equity ideas. In bonds, we lock in gains on US inflation-
protected bonds as inflation nears a peak.

mid-cycle market dynamics. Last week, as the US equity 
market volatility index VIX surged above 25, we partially dialled 
up risk with a tactical idea on the more diversified US IT sector. 
Staying tactical within a diversified allocation. Equity 
volatility following last week’s Democratic clean sweep in the 
US elections has subsided. Questions about the pace of AI data 
centre buildout are likely to linger until Democrats take office in 
in January. Nvidia’s turmoil adds uncertainty. We remain 
broadly diversified and balanced in our core allocation across 
assets, sector and regions, while seeking tactical opportunities. 
History suggests sharp selloffs are followed by near-term 
volatility, particularly during macroeconomic stress. However, 
the balance of historical outcomes has typically shifted strongly 
to the upside over a three- to six-month horizon. Besides, 
historically, US equity markets have delivered the strongest 12-
month returns after opposition clean sweeps in the mid-terms. 
Rotation driving US IT sector recovery: As volatility eases, 
our opportunistic US IT equities idea, launched a week ago, has 
performed well, despite some significantly negative company 
specific developments. Attractive valuations support further 
upside. Investors are rotating within IT from semiconductors, 
affected by concerns over the pace of AI data centre buildout 
and Nvidia’s troubles, towards software and hardware 
providers. The latter have strong cash flows and balance sheets 
and are likely to benefit from AI monetisation. Together, they 
account for more than 60% of the IT sector (see page 4).

Remain constructive on US healthcare. US healthcare 
earnings are projected to grow almost 23% in 2027, the second-
fastest among MSCI US sectors. We see scope for further 
upside in the sector as innovation underpins long-term earnings 
growth. With the market rotating away from concentrated AI 
exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical demand profile 
offers a compelling exposure as a diversifier (see page 5). 
Outside the US, Switzerland and India offer diversified 
returns. The Swiss equity market consists of 41% healthcare, 
19% financial services and 14% consumer staples, providing a 
defensive alternative to investors seeking to diversify away 
from tech-heavy exposure. The Indian equity market has been 
classified as an “anti-AI” trade. Historically narrow valuation 
premiums vs. EMs, low foreign investor positioning and c. 15% 
expected 2027 earnings growth make it an attractive diversifier. 
Strong jobs and inflation reports cement December Fed 
rate hike. The US economy added 165,000 jobs in October, 
more than twice the 70,000 consensus estimate. The 
unemployment rate fell to 4.1%. Meanwhile, headline and core 
inflation in October, at 0.4% and 0.3% m/m, respectively, 
exceeded expectations as elevated fuel prices, ‘techflation’ and 
earlier tariffs fed into directly exposed sectors, including food, 
utilities and transportation services. A healthy job market keeps 
the Fed focused on lowering inflation, cementing the prospect 
of a December rate hike, potentially followed by another in Q1 
2027. A strong economy and elevated interest rates support our 
overweight allocation to US financial sector equities. 
Locking in gains from our bullish US inflation-protected 
bonds idea. While near-term price pressures remain elevated, 
we expect inflation to ease with energy costs over the coming 
year. Republicans’ heavy losses in the US mid-term elections 
increase pressure on the Trump administration to end the Iran 
conflict. One-year US inflation expectations have slumped 
alongside oil prices. A potential slowdown in the pace of AI 
buildout is also likely to ease techflation. Meanwhile, Fed rate 
hikes should further tighten financial conditions, tempering 
price pressures. This disinflationary outlook makes the risk-
reward for US inflation-protected bonds less compelling. We 
close the idea, locking in a 4.4% gain (page 6). 
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

What does history tell us about the aftermath of a single-
day sell-off of 4% or more in the S&P500 index?

Our view: The S&P500 fell 4.36% on 5 November 2026, a rare daily 
move of 3.8 standard deviations. Since 2009, there have been only

24 occasions when the index declined by 4% or more in a single

session. The immediate aftermath of such sell-offs has historically

been mixed. One-month returns ranged from around -15% to

+23%, with positive returns in 70% of cases. The weaker

outcomes were concentrated around periods of significant macro

stress, including the tail end of the 2008 Global Financial Crisis

(GFC), the 2020 COVID-19 pandemic and the 2022 monetary policy

tightening cycle. 
The historical picture becomes considerably more constructive

beyond the first month. All 24 episodes generated positive three-
month returns, ranging from roughly +0.5% to +38%, while six-
month returns were positive in 96% of cases, with gains reaching 
as much as +49%. Overall, history suggests sharp sell-offs can be 
followed by near-term volatility, particularly during periods of broader 
macro stress. However, the balance of historical outcomes has 
shifted strongly to the upside over a 3-6-month horizon. 
—  Maggie Au Yeung, Quantitative Analyst 
 o
— Francis Lim, Senior Quantitative Strategist
Where do you see the most favourable risk-reward within

the US technology sector? 
Our view: We favour high-quality Big Tech companies with 
strong balance sheets and prefer diversified exposure through 
our US Information Technology (IT) opportunistic idea. 
Rationale: The recent pullback in US semiconductor stocks has 
improved their risk-reward profile. Earnings for these companies are 
forecast to grow by 65% in 2027, the fastest rate among technology 
subsectors. Despite this outlook, recent regulatory and corporate 
governance concerns are weighing on sentiment. Potential state-
level restrictions on data centre development following the recent 
US mid-term election results could weigh on semiconductor demand 
growth. However, we believe these risks are manageable and 
continued AI adoption should support demand for AI inference. We 
prefer diversified exposure to quality semiconductor 
companies with durable demand and strong volume growth. 
To navigate volatility in the US technology sector, we favour Big Tech 
companies with strong balance sheets and resilient cash generation, 
as they are better positioned to sustain AI investments without 
relying heavily on external financing. Our US IT opportunistic idea 
combines these preferences through exposure to semiconductors, 
software and hardware, allowing investors to participate in a 
potential recovery across a broader range of earnings drivers.
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d)

Do fundamental drivers support a constructive view on the 
US healthcare sector?

Our view: We remain constructive on US healthcare, supported

by its defensive characteristics and robust earnings growth.

Rationale: US healthcare earnings are projected to grow by 22.5% 
in 2027, the second-fastest among MSCI US sectors, driven by 
improving biopharma earnings. The US healthcare sector's 
valuation gap against the broader market has been recovering from 
a steep historical discount a year ago. The sector's forward price-to-

earnings (P/E) ratio of c. 20x now exceeds the broader US market’s

c. 18.4x, but we see scope for the valuation to be supported amid

investor demand for defensive growth. The sector’s broad legislative

policy overhang has materially faded following the US election cycle,

while targeted regulatory and oversight risks remain on the watchlist.

Healthcare innovation also underpins structural growth. Wider

adoption of obesity and diabetes treatments, alongside advances in

oncology treatments, medical devices and robotic surgery, should

support long-term earnings growth. With the market rotating away

from concentrated AI exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical 
demand profile presents compelling opportunities for investors 
seeking both defensiveness and growth in the near term.
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst
What is your outlook for China’s Big Tech sector? 
Our view: We remain positive on China’s Big Tech sector, 
consistent with our Overweight stance on the country’s IT and 
communication services sector. 
Rationale: Valuations across China’s technology sector remain 
inexpensive at 14.2x, with investor attention centred on whether AI-
driven revenue can offset elevated AI-related spending, which is 
currently compressing margins. Elevated AI capex and negative 
free cash flow remain key investor concerns in the Q3 earnings 
season. Top-line momentum also remains uneven: soft domestic 
consumption continues to weigh on e-commerce, advertising and 
local services, whereas gaming and cloud computing are 
delivering resilient growth. The broader narrative is steadily 
transitioning towards commercialisation, with rapid enterprise 
penetration by AI workplace agents and upcoming AI model 
launches shifting the market focus towards active deployment and 
conversion to paid usage. Evolving regulatory frameworks 
covering AI governance, agent self-regulation, tax scrutiny and 
compliance requirements remain important variables influencing 
medium-term margin trajectories. Ultimately, valuation support, 
gradual monetisation of AI capabilities and resilient core 
segments support our Overweight positioning.
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

grow 22.5% in 2027 – the second-fastest among

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Why are you closing your US Treasury Inflation-Protected

Securities (TIPS) opportunistic idea? 
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish US TIPS 
position, as the risk-reward profile has become less compelling.

The trade returned 4.4% since its initiation on 11 April 2025.

Rationale: We initiated an opportunistic bullish position in US TIPS

on 11 April 2025 as an oil and inflation hedge, expecting geopolitical

uncertainty to keep inflation expectations elevated. The risk-reward,

however, has since become less compelling, as hawkish Fed 
commentary, easing geopolitical risks and a potential slowdown in 
the pace of AI buildout are now likely to ease broader inflation and 
‘techflation’ pressures. We have therefore closed the opportunistic

idea, realising a gain of 4.4%.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist
How do falling oil prices and October’s US inflation data 
affect your Fed and bond market outlook? 
Our view: Our base case envisages two further 25bps Fed hikes 
by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 2027 if energy prices 
remain consistently lower and growth trajectory normalises. We 
favour income over chasing duration gains, focusing on higher-
quality corporate bonds with 3-7-year maturities. 
Rationale: Lower oil prices have a two-sided effect: they ease 
inflationary pressures and support growth by boosting purchasing 
power and lowering business costs. Cheaper energy, therefore, 
does not automatically justify Fed easing. Our base case envisages 
two further 25bps hikes by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 
2027 if energy prices remain consistently lower. Sustained 
moderation in core inflation would challenge the near-term tightening 
path. However, October’s US consumer inflation print reinforced our 
assessment that each Fed meeting now remains a ‘live’ decision. 
With the US 10-year government bond yield already within our 5.0-
5.25% 3-month range, we favour income over chasing duration 
gains. Credit performance has diverged: Developed Market high-
yield bonds have outperformed both investment-grade credit and 
government bonds. Credit still offers attractive income, and we retain 
our preference for 3-7-year maturities, although heavy primary 
issuance remains a counterweight. We favour selective carry, with a 
preference for higher-quality corporate bonds. These include bonds 
issued by leading AI hyperscalers, where risk-reward has become 
more attractive. Our emphasis is on generating steady income, 
rather than relying on further declines in bond yields or spreads.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist
While real (net-of-inflation) yields remain elevated, 
the inflation-protection function of US TIPS has 
diminished as inflation expectations ease

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d)
Does the USD’s rally have further to run?

Our view: Further upside appears limited. Stronger-than-

expected US core inflation in October and hawkish Fed

comments have pushed the US Dollar Index (DXY) above 103.

However, markets already price in more Fed tightening than we

expect. We see the DXY returning to its 101.00-102.95 range in

the near term, before gradually weakening towards our 12-

month forecast of 98.5.

Rationale: US core consumer prices rose 0.3% m/m in October,

above the 0.2% m/m expected, meeting Fed Governor Waller’s

threshold for considering a 50bps December rate hike. This pushed

US government bond yields higher, supporting the USD rally. 
However, futures now imply a Fed rate peak of 4.50-4.75% by

mid-2027, compared with our expectation of 4.25-4.50% following

two 25bps hikes, one in December and the second in H1 2027. We

expect the Fed to begin easing in H2 2027. As market expectations 
gradually converge towards our view, USD support from US bond 
yields should weaken.
Meanwhile, falling oil and petrol prices should help ease headline 
inflation in the coming months, reducing pressure for further Fed 
tightening. This reinforces our expectation of limited USD upside, 
although persistent services inflation remains a key risk.
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist
Do you expect USD/JPY to sustainably break out above 
160 in the near term?
Our view: We see USD/JPY rangebound with a downside bias, 
as growing BoJ tightening expectations and potential policy 
convergence will support gradual JPY appreciation.
Rationale: Renewed tech optimism has encouraged the re-
establishment of carry trades, providing support to the pair lately. 
Meanwhile, US core inflation beat expectations for a third 
consecutive month in October, reinforcing expectations for a 
December Fed hike. Further upside inflation surprises could widen 
the US-Japan interest rate differential and push USD/JPY higher. 
However, the risk of intervention by Japan’s Ministry of Finance 
is likely to rise materially around 160, limiting the scope for 
sustained upside. On the downside, a 25bps BoJ hike to 1.50%, 
accompanied by clear signals of consecutive hikes in March and 
June 2027, would be a significant yen-positive catalyst. A more non-
linear downside scenario would be a disorderly equity sell-off, which 
could trigger rapid JPY appreciation through carry-trade unwinding. 
We see scope for the pair to test support towards 156 in the 
coming weeks.
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist
US dollar index’s rally above 103 looks stretched

## Document 397: THINK Ahead_ Your questions, answered

Source PDF: all report pdf/THINK Ahead_ Your questions, answered.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Our Q&A explores where ING’s Global Outlook diﬀers from
market expectations, from the path for interest rates and
inﬂation to US debt, European ﬁscal risks, wages and gold

Carsten Brzeski, Michiel Tukker, Peter Vanden Houte, James
Knightley and Ewa Manthey
Our Q&A examines how ING’s Global Outlook diﬀers from market expectations
and where we see the biggest disconnects
What is the biggest disconnect today
between what the market is pricing and
ING’s Global Outlook?
Our base case scenario is more dovish and benign than markets
are currently pricing in. We expect oil prices to fall further, easing
inﬂationary pressures and allowing central banks to stop hiking
rates after a ﬁnal rate hike in December.
Due to weaker household balance sheets, we see only limited pass-
through from higher energy prices to core inﬂation, with companies
instead having to squeeze proﬁt margins. In this scenario, major
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

central banks could even start considering rate cuts towards the
end of 2027.
Is it normal for markets to be pricing in
virtually no rate cuts? Beyond 12 months,
interest rate expectations are virtually ﬂat.
How does that compare to historic rate hike
cycles?
Compared to today’s curve, in 2008 and early 2023 markets priced
in a much larger decline in rates beyond the near-term peak. The
current curve is much more similar in shape to the 2007 hiking

cycle.
The current curve is more similar to the
2007 hiking cycle than the one in 2023
Based on these prior hiking episodes, we foresee two conditions
that would warrant a more humped yield curve, i.e. a curve with a
lower terminal policy rate. The ﬁrst is a material slowdown in the
growth outlook. The most extreme version of this is the 2008 policy
error by the European Central Bank – we all know what happened
after the ECB decided to hike in 2008. So far, the growth outlook

remains robust, and we do not see this as a likely path for 2027.
The second trigger of a more humped-shaped yield curve would be
a decline in inﬂation risks. Once inﬂation peaked in 2023, markets
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies

### Page 3

started repricing a lower neutral rate. In the near term, the curve still
held a hiking bias, but markets started pricing in a lower ECB rate
further out in the future. We believe this is the more likely path rates
will take and therefore expect a repricing lower by mid-2027.
Does Europe have a 'K-shaped' economy
like the US? And is there scope for
household savings to shield consumer
spending from higher energy prices in 2027?
Lower-income households generally suﬀer more from higher

energy prices than higher-income households, as they spend a
larger share of their income on energy and food. The energy shock
could therefore produce a more K-shaped consumption pattern.
However, Europe’s extensive social safety net provides important
protection for lower-income groups. Indeed, the consumer-
conﬁdence gap between the highest and lowest income quartiles
has not widened in 2026. Quite the opposite.
Since energy prices began rising, the savings rate has fallen,

cushioning the negative impact on consumption. However, with
households’ unemployment expectations increasing, it would be
overly optimistic to assume that the eurozone household savings
rate will continue to decline.
US growth has remained strong, but
deﬁcits are rising and a Democrat-run
Congress could put pressure on data centre
construction. Is the US heading towards a
debt crisis?
The US's ﬁscal position is poor. The government is currently
spending 4% of GDP on interest costs while running a primary

deﬁcit (tax revenue less departmental and programme spending) of
2.5% of GDP. The mid-term election results mean the primary deﬁcit
is pretty much locked in at 2.5% of GDP, with President Trump likely
vetoing any Democrat tax and spending policies approved in
Congress.
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
